As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means the werewolves didn't kill someone who was already protected. I think we should focus on logic and not just suspicion. Let's hear from others before we target Frank.
  Frank: I investigated Olivia last night, and she was indeed a Villager. While I don't have information on others yet, I agree we should use logic over suspicion. However, if Frank was acting unusually, it's worth keeping an eye on him. Let’s hear from others before making a decision.
  Uma: I think we need to consider who had the opportunity to kill Olivia. Since the werewolves act at night, we should look at who was awake or could have been involved in the kill. === Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Jack, Bob, Frank, Uma, Wendy, Iris, Noah, Grace, Zoe, Yara, Paul

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Jack: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who was acting suspiciously during the night. I don't trust Frank—he kept looking around nervously. We should investigate him first.
  Bob: I agree that we need to be careful, but I don't think we should jump to conclusions. As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means the werewolves didn't kill someone who was already protected. I think we should focus on logic and not just suspicion. Let's hear from others before we target Frank.
  Frank: I investigated Olivia last night, and she was indeed a Villager. While I don't have information on others yet, I agree we should use logic over suspicion. However, if Frank was acting unusually, it's worth keeping an eye on him. Let’s hear from others before making a decision.
  Uma: I think we need to consider who had the opportunity to kill Olivia. Since the werewolves act at night, we should look at who was awake or could have been involved in the kill. Frank mentioned he investigated Olivia, but if he's a werewolf, he could have lied about that. I think we should question Frank further before making a decision.
  Wendy: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. If Frank is the Seer, he would have investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what he said. However, we should not assume he's innocent just because he has information. Let’s keep pressure on him and others who acted suspiciously, but also consider that the werewolves might be trying to frame someone. We need more clues before we vote.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to focus on logic and the facts we have. Frank claims he investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what we know. However, we should not let that absolve him of suspicion. The werewolves could be trying to manipulate us by making someone look trustworthy. I believe we should continue gathering information and not eliminate someone based solely on suspicion. Let’s keep an open mind and look for more clues.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Jack, Bob, Frank, Uma, Wendy, Iris, Noah, Grace, Zoe, Yara, Paul

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Jack: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who was acting suspiciously during the night. I don't trust Frank—he kept looking around nervously. We should investigate him first.
  Bob: I agree that we need to be careful, but I don't think we should jump to conclusions. As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means the werewolves didn't kill someone who was already protected. I think we should focus on logic and not just suspicion. Let's hear from others before we target Frank.
  Frank: I investigated Olivia last night, and she was indeed a Villager. While I don't have information on others yet, I agree we should use logic over suspicion. However, if Frank was acting unusually, it's worth keeping an eye on him. Let’s hear from others before making a decision.
  Uma: I think we need to consider who had the opportunity to kill Olivia. Since the werewolves act at night, we should look at who was awake or could have been involved in the kill. Frank mentioned he investigated Olivia, but if he's a werewolf, he could have lied about that. I think we should question Frank further before making a decision.
  Wendy: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. If Frank is the Seer, he would have investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what he said. However, we should not assume he's innocent just because he has information. Let’s keep pressure on him and others who acted suspiciously, but also consider that the werewolves might be trying to frame someone. We need more clues before we vote.
  Iris: I think we need to focus on logic and the facts we have. Frank claims he investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what we know. However, we should not let that absolve him of suspicion. The werewolves could be trying to manipulate us by making someone look trustworthy. I believe we should continue gathering information and not eliminate someone based solely on suspicion. Let’s keep an open mind and look for more clues.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to be strategic. As a werewolf, I know Olivia was killed by us, and Frank's claim about investigating her is likely true. However, I also know that the werewolves are still hidden. I don’t trust anyone fully, but I believe we should focus on those who are pushing too hard to eliminate others without proof. Let’s keep our eyes open and not let fear drive our decisions.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to be strategic. As a werewolf, I know Olivia was killed by us, and Frank's claim about investigating her is likely true. However, I also know that the werewolves are still hidden. I don’t trust anyone fully, but I believe we should focus on those who are pushing too hard to eliminate others without proof. Let’s keep our eyes open and not let fear drive our decisions."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Jack, Bob, Frank, Uma, Wendy, Iris, Noah, Grace, Zoe, Yara, Paul

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Jack: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who was acting suspiciously during the night. I don't trust Frank—he kept looking around nervously. We should investigate him first.
  Bob: I agree that we need to be careful, but I don't think we should jump to conclusions. As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means the werewolves didn't kill someone who was already protected. I think we should focus on logic and not just suspicion. Let's hear from others before we target Frank.
  Frank: I investigated Olivia last night, and she was indeed a Villager. While I don't have information on others yet, I agree we should use logic over suspicion. However, if Frank was acting unusually, it's worth keeping an eye on him. Let’s hear from others before making a decision.
  Uma: I think we need to consider who had the opportunity to kill Olivia. Since the werewolves act at night, we should look at who was awake or could have been involved in the kill. Frank mentioned he investigated Olivia, but if he's a werewolf, he could have lied about that. I think we should question Frank further before making a decision.
  Wendy: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. If Frank is the Seer, he would have investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what he said. However, we should not assume he's innocent just because he has information. Let’s keep pressure on him and others who acted suspiciously, but also consider that the werewolves might be trying to frame someone. We need more clues before we vote.
  Iris: I think we need to focus on logic and the facts we have. Frank claims he investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what we know. However, we should not let that absolve him of suspicion. The werewolves could be trying to manipulate us by making someone look trustworthy. I believe we should continue gathering information and not eliminate someone based solely on suspicion. Let’s keep an open mind and look for more clues.
  Noah: I think we need to be strategic. As a werewolf, I know Olivia was killed by us, and Frank's claim about investigating her is likely true. However, I also know that the werewolves are still hidden. I don’t trust anyone fully, but I believe we should focus on those who are pushing too hard to eliminate others without proof. Let’s keep our eyes open and not let fear drive our decisions.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to be careful not to let fear or suspicion lead us astray. Olivia was a Villager, and while we need to find the werewolves, we should focus on logic and evidence. I don’t have any reason to believe Frank is lying about his investigation, and I think we should keep an open mind until we have more information. Let’s not eliminate someone without clear proof.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to be careful not to let fear or suspicion lead us astray. Olivia was a Villager, and while we need to find the werewolves, we should focus on logic and evidence. I don’t have any reason to believe Frank is lying about his investigation, and I think we should keep an open mind until we have more information. Let’s not eliminate someone without clear proof.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Jack, Bob, Frank, Uma, Wendy, Iris, Noah, Grace, Zoe, Yara, Paul

  Day 1: Olivia (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Jack: Olivia was eliminated on Day 1, and since she was a villager, that means the werewolves are still among us. I think we need to look at who was acting suspiciously during the night. I don't trust Frank—he kept looking around nervously. We should investigate him first.
  Bob: I agree that we need to be careful, but I don't think we should jump to conclusions. As the Doctor, I was able to save someone last night, which means the werewolves didn't kill someone who was already protected. I think we should focus on logic and not just suspicion. Let's hear from others before we target Frank.
  Frank: I investigated Olivia last night, and she was indeed a Villager. While I don't have information on others yet, I agree we should use logic over suspicion. However, if Frank was acting unusually, it's worth keeping an eye on him. Let’s hear from others before making a decision.
  Uma: I think we need to consider who had the opportunity to kill Olivia. Since the werewolves act at night, we should look at who was awake or could have been involved in the kill. Frank mentioned he investigated Olivia, but if he's a werewolf, he could have lied about that. I think we should question Frank further before making a decision.
  Wendy: I think we need to be cautious but also logical. If Frank is the Seer, he would have investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what he said. However, we should not assume he's innocent just because he has information. Let’s keep pressure on him and others who acted suspiciously, but also consider that the werewolves might be trying to frame someone. We need more clues before we vote.
  Iris: I think we need to focus on logic and the facts we have. Frank claims he investigated Olivia and confirmed she was a Villager, which aligns with what we know. However, we should not let that absolve him of suspicion. The werewolves could be trying to manipulate us by making someone look trustworthy. I believe we should continue gathering information and not eliminate someone based solely on suspicion. Let’s keep an open mind and look for more clues.
  Noah: I think we need to be strategic. As a werewolf, I know Olivia was killed by us, and Frank's claim about investigating her is likely true.
